=== FILE: AGENTS.md ===
# GenOps — Cascading Specification Pipeline

This project uses **GenOps**, a separation-of-concerns agent-native pipeline engine that decomposes complex software work into isolated, cascading specification stages. Each stage is a scoped native skill backed by a deterministic CLI helper.

## Pipeline Overview

```
/genops-prd ──cascade──> /genops-hld ──cascade──> /genops-adr ──cascade──> /genops-lld ──cascade──> /genops-code
     │                       │                       │                       │                       │
     v                       v                       v                       v                       v
docs/prd/               docs/hld/           docs/architecture/          docs/lld/               src/**/*
```

The pipeline is defined declaratively in `genops.yaml`. Each stage produces files into its output directory with standardized YAML frontmatter headers.

## Document Organization

Files use **domain-split** naming: `{STAGE}-{NNN}-{descriptive-slug}.md`

```
docs/
├── prd/PRD-001-taskflow-requirements.md     ← Single domain = 1 file
├── hld/HLD-001-system-architecture.md       ← Single domain = 1 file
├── architecture/
│   ├── ADR-001-go-language.md               ← One per decision
│   ├── ADR-002-sqlite-storage.md
│   └── ADR-003-cobra-cli-framework.md
├── lld/LLD-001-taskflow-design.md           ← Single domain = 1 file
└── code/
    ├── CODE-001-core-domain.md              ← Multiple files per domain
    ├── CODE-002-storage-layer.md
    └── CODE-003-cli-commands.md
```

For multi-domain projects (e.g., e-commerce: catalog, cart, payments):

```
docs/prd/PRD-001-product-catalog.md
docs/prd/PRD-002-shopping-cart.md
docs/prd/PRD-003-checkout-payment.md
```

The domain slug in the filename IS the agent's navigation system. `grep docs/ payment` finds all files across all layers related to "payment". Versioning is handled by git.

<!-- GENOPS:START — managed by genops-init, edit pipeline stages via genops.yaml -->

## GenOps Cascading Specification Pipeline (Software Specification Pipeline)

This project uses **GenOps**, a separation-of-concerns pipeline engine that decomposes complex software work into isolated, cascading specification stages backed by deterministic LF-normalized SHA-256 tracking.

### Available Stage Commands

| Command | Scope | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `/genops-prd` | Product Requirements | Product vision, user stories, success metrics, scope boundaries |
| `/genops-hld` | High-Level Design | System topology, component boundaries, data flow, integration points |
| `/genops-adr` | Architecture Decisions | Architectural trade-offs, technology selection, design pattern choices |
| `/genops-lld` | Low-Level Design | Class diagrams, database schemas, API contracts, module structure |
| `/genops-code` | Implementation | Scaffold project from LLD design using scaffold templates |
| `/genops` | Pipeline Engine | Orchestrate pipeline, check health status |

### Flow Modes

| Mode | Command Example | Description |
|---|---|---|
| **SoC (Default)** | `/genops-prd` | One stage at a time. Solicits human approval before offering next. |
| **Targeted** | `/genops-prd --domain <slug>` | Scopes execution or modification exclusively to specified domain. |
| **Flow** | `/genops-prd --flow` | Completes stage, then automatically cascades to next stage. |
| **Nonstop** | `/genops-prd --nonstop` | Runs full cascade with approval gates at each stage. |
| **Incremental** | `/genops --from adr --domain <slug>` | Starts incremental cascade for a single domain. |
| **Status** | `/genops --status` | Shows live pipeline health and stale downstream flags. |

### Separation of Concerns Protocol

Each stage execution adheres strictly to:
1. **Pre-flight**: Verifies prerequisites exist and are approved.
2. **Context**: Loads upstream requirements and domain terms.
3. **Drafting**: Generates modular `{STAGE}-{NNN}-{slug}.md` documents with standardized YAML frontmatter.
4. **Approval**: Hard gate requiring explicit confirmation before transition.
5. **State Recording**: Updates `docs/.genops-state.json` (v2.0) and logs immutable events to `docs/.genops-events.jsonl`.

<!-- GENOPS:END -->

=== FILE: CONTRIBUTING.md ===
# Contributing to GenOps

GenOps follows its own pipeline. All contributions should flow through the same specification stages and quality standards that GenOps provides.

## Contribution Workflow

1. **Fork** the repository
2. **Propose** your change as a specification (`/genops-prd`)
3. **Design** the approach if architectural (`/genops-hld`, `/genops-adr`)
4. **Implement** via skill changes, presets, schemas, or engine improvements
5. **Validate** configuration and schemas via `python .agents/scripts/genops.py validate`
6. **Submit** a pull request with the specification documents in the PR description

## What to Contribute

- **New pipeline presets** — Add `.agents/presets/<name>.yaml` with matching templates, schemas, and skills
- **Scaffold templates** — Add new tech stacks in `.agents/scaffolds/<name>/` with valid `STRUCTURE.yaml`
- **Skill improvements** — Enhance protocol steps, interview questions, or validators
- **Templates** — Improve output structures while retaining standardized YAML frontmatter
- **Engine enhancements** — Extend `.agents/scripts/genops.py` (CLI, hashing, AST analysis, anti-drift gates)
- **Documentation & Examples** — Fix errors, add tutorials, expand multi-domain guides

## Development & Skill Guidelines

- **Namespacing:** Skills must use the `genops-` prefix namespace.
- **Protocol:** Follow `genops-stage`: PRE-FLIGHT → LOAD → DOMAINS → CHECK → INTERVIEW → GENERATE → VALIDATE → PRESENT → APPROVE → RECORD → TRANSITION.
- **Token Efficiency:** Keep skill files concise (<100 lines) for hot-path agent performance.
- **Frontmatter:** YAML frontmatter descriptions must start with "Use when...".
- **Hard Gates:** Always include `<HARD-GATE>` blocks for critical verification and approval gates.
- **Machine-Readable Specs:** All generated templates must include standardized YAML frontmatter headers.

## Testing Your Changes

1. **Run Engine Validation:**
   ```bash
   python .agents/scripts/genops.py validate
   ```
2. **Run Pipeline Simulation:**
   - Execute a demo project across all stages (`PRD → HLD → ADR → LLD → Code`).
   - Verify state recording in `docs/.genops-state.json` (v2.0) and event logging in `docs/.genops-events.jsonl`.
3. **Test Staleness & Reactivity:**
   - Modify an upstream document, run `python .agents/scripts/genops.py status`, and verify downstream stages are flagged stale.
4. **Test Scaffolding:**
   - Run `python .agents/scripts/genops.py scaffold` to verify output directory trees, casing transforms, and entity stubs.

## Code of Conduct

- Be respectful, constructive, and assume good intent.
- Follow the GenOps SoC (Separation of Concerns) principle: address one topic per discussion.

## License

By contributing, you agree that your contributions will be licensed under the MIT License.

=== FILE: genops.yaml ===
# GenOps Pipeline Configuration — Software Specification
#
# Preset: software-spec
# Use with: /genops-init --preset software-spec
# Stages: prd → hld → adr → lld → code

pipeline:
  name: "Software Specification Pipeline"
  stages:
    - id: prd
      name: "Product Requirements"
      focus: "Product vision, user stories, success metrics, scope boundaries"
      requires: []
      outputs:
        - "docs/prd/"
      file_pattern: "PRD-{NNN}-{slug}.md"
      template: "prd/PRD-domain.md.template"
      next:
        - hld

    - id: hld
      name: "High-Level Design"
      focus: "System topology, component boundaries, data flow, integration points"
      requires:
        - "docs/prd/"
      outputs:
        - "docs/hld/"
      file_pattern: "HLD-{NNN}-{slug}.md"
      template: "hld/HLD-domain.md.template"
      next:
        - adr

    - id: adr
      name: "Architecture Decisions"
      focus: "Architectural trade-offs, technology selection, design pattern choices"
      requires:
        - "docs/hld/"
      outputs:
        - "docs/architecture/"
      file_pattern: "ADR-{NNN}-{slug}.md"
      template: "adr/ADR.md.template"
      next:
        - lld

    - id: lld
      name: "Low-Level Design"
      focus: "Class diagrams, database schemas, API contracts, module structure"
      requires:
        - "docs/hld/"
        - "docs/architecture/"
      outputs:
        - "docs/lld/"
      file_pattern: "LLD-{NNN}-{slug}.md"
      template: "lld/LLD-domain.md.template"
      next:
        - code

    - id: code
      name: "Implementation"
      focus: "Scaffold project from LLD design using scaffold templates"
      requires:
        - "docs/lld/"
      outputs:
        - "src/"
      next: []

  validation_rules:
    - from: prd
      to: hld
      rule: "Verify each Key Capability from PRD maps to ≥1 HLD component or data flow"
    - from: hld
      to: adr
      rule: "Verify all Technology items marked 'Needs ADR' in HLD have matching ADRs"
    - from: adr
      to: lld
      rule: "Verify each ADR decision is reflected in LLD entities, schema, or contracts"
    - from: hld
      to: lld
      rule: "Verify LLD module structure matches HLD component descriptions"
    - from: lld
      to: code
      rule: "Verify Code scaffold modules match LLD Project Structure modules. All entities have stubs."

=== FILE: README.md ===
# GenOps — Agent-Native Specification Pipeline (v3.0 Super Skills)

**Declarative. Cascading. Executable. Agent-First.**

GenOps is a separation-of-concerns pipeline engine that decomposes complex software work into isolated, cascading specification stages. Each stage is a native agent skill backed by a deterministic, zero-dependency engine (`.agents/scripts/genops.py`), a Model Context Protocol (MCP) server, and an active Living Memory Compactor (`CONTEXT.md`).

```mermaid
%%{init: {'theme': 'base', 'themeVariables': { 'fontFamily': 'Inter, system-ui, sans-serif', 'lineColor': '#64748b', 'primaryTextColor': '#0f172a' }}}%%
flowchart LR
    subgraph P1 ["1. Requirements"]
        direction TB
        CMD_PRD["/genops-prd<br/><b>Principal PM Persona</b>"]
        DOC_PRD[("docs/prd/<br/>PRD-*.md")]
        CMD_PRD --> DOC_PRD
    end

    subgraph P2 ["2. High-Level Design"]
        direction TB
        CMD_HLD["/genops-hld<br/><b>Principal Architect Persona</b>"]
        DOC_HLD[("docs/hld/<br/>HLD-*.md")]
        CMD_HLD --> DOC_HLD
    end

    subgraph P3 ["3. Architecture Decisions"]
        direction TB
        CMD_ADR["/genops-adr<br/><b>Staff Systems Engineer</b>"]
        DOC_ADR[("docs/architecture/<br/>ADR-*.md")]
        CMD_ADR --> DOC_ADR
    end

    subgraph P4 ["4. Low-Level Design"]
        direction TB
        CMD_LLD["/genops-lld<br/><b>Lead Engineer Persona</b>"]
        DOC_LLD[("docs/lld/<br/>LLD-*.md")]
        CMD_LLD --> DOC_LLD
    end

    subgraph P5 ["5. Implementation"]
        direction TB
        CMD_CODE["/genops-code<br/><b>Principal Software Engineer</b>"]
        OUT_SRC[("src/<br/>Clean Architecture & TDD")]
        CMD_CODE --> OUT_SRC
    end

    DOC_PRD ==>|"Cascade"| CMD_HLD
    DOC_HLD ==>|"Cascade"| CMD_ADR
    DOC_ADR ==>|"Cascade"| CMD_LLD
    DOC_LLD ==>|"Scaffold"| CMD_CODE

    classDef stageNode fill:#f8fafc,stroke:#2563eb,stroke-width:1.5px,color:#0f172a,rx:8px,ry:8px;
    classDef docNode fill:#ffffff,stroke:#64748b,stroke-width:1px,color:#334155,rx:6px,ry:6px;
    classDef codeNode fill:#f0fdf4,stroke:#16a34a,stroke-width:1.5px,color:#14532d,rx:8px,ry:8px;
    
    class CMD_PRD,CMD_HLD,CMD_ADR,CMD_LLD stageNode;
    class DOC_PRD,DOC_HLD,DOC_ADR,DOC_LLD docNode;
    class CMD_CODE,OUT_SRC codeNode;
```

---

## Why GenOps Super Skills (v3.0)

AI coding agents are powerful but prone to context drift, premature optimization, and mixed concerns. GenOps transforms AI agents into specialized Staff-level engineers with executable guardrails:

- **Specialized Cognitive Personas** — Principal PM (BDD & Anti-Features), Principal Systems Architect (C4 & Failure Domains), Staff Systems Engineer (5-Vector Weighted ADR Matrices), Lead Systems Engineer (DDD & Executable Contracts), and Principal Software Engineer (Clean Architecture & TDD).
- **Living Memory Compactor (`CONTEXT.md`)** — Active summarization engine that automatically extracts personas, glossaries, accepted ADR choices, and architectural constraints upon every stage approval.
- **Socratic Architectural Challenger** — Pushes back against premature microservice complexity, interrogates network failure boundaries, and demands numeric SLO targets.
- **Multi-Perspective Critic Passes** — Evaluates every draft across Security (STRIDE), Resilience (Backoffs/Circuit Breakers), Observability (OpenTelemetry/APM), and Interface consistency before presentation.
- **Executable Technical Artifacts** — Generates PostgreSQL 16+ DDL migrations, OpenAPI 3.1 contracts, Go/Python port interfaces, and typed domain aggregate invariants.
- **TDD-First Polyglot Scaffolding** — `/genops-code` generates Clean / Hexagonal folder structures (`domain/`, `ports/`, `adapters/`, `handlers/`, `tests/`) across Go, Python, React, Rust, and Node.js with failing test suites.
- **Compiler-in-the-Loop Diagnostics (`genops verify`)** — Validates syntax, types, and package boundaries before prompting the user.
- **Multi-Domain Pipelines** — First-class presets for **Software Specification**, **Systematic Research** (`lit-review` $\to$ `hypothesis` $\to$ `experiment` $\to$ `report`), and **Strategic Design** (`brief` $\to$ `wireframes` $\to$ `mockups` $\to$ `prototype`).

---

## Architecture & System Topology

```mermaid
%%{init: {'theme': 'base', 'themeVariables': { 'fontFamily': 'Inter, system-ui, sans-serif', 'lineColor': '#64748b', 'primaryTextColor': '#0f172a' }}}%%

    subgraph Agents ["Universal AI Coding Agent Layer"]
        direction LR
        AG1["Claude Code"]

        AG3["Antigravity / Gemini"]
        AG4["GitHub Copilot"]
        AG5["Windsurf"]
        AG6["Aider / OpenCode"]
    end

    subgraph Engine ["GenOps Control Plane & Deterministic Engine (genops.py)"]
        direction TB
        CLI["CLI Interface<br/>(init, validate, status, scaffold, compact, verify)"]
        MCP["Native MCP Server<br/>(JSON-RPC stdio)"]
        HASHER["LF-Normalized SHA-256 Hasher"]
        LOCK["State Lockfile<br/>(.genops.lock)"]
        COMPACTOR["Living Memory Compactor<br/>(CONTEXT.md)"]
        RULES["Cross-Layer Rules Checker"]
        DRIFT["CI/CD Anti-Drift Gate"]
        
        CLI <--> LOCK
        MCP <--> LOCK
        LOCK --> HASHER
        LOCK --> COMPACTOR
        LOCK --> RULES
        LOCK --> DRIFT
    end

    subgraph Specs ["Docs-as-Context (ContextOps) Specification Layer"]
        direction TB
        FRONTMATTER["Standard YAML Frontmatter Headers"]
        DAG["Lineage DAG Graph (.genops-graph.json)"]
        STATE["State v2.0 (.genops-state.json)"]
        EVENTS["Immutable Audit Trail (.genops-events.jsonl)"]
        CONTEXT["Active Living Memory (.agents/context/CONTEXT.md)"]
        
        FRONTMATTER --> DAG
        DAG --> STATE
        STATE --> EVENTS
        STATE --> CONTEXT
    end

    subgraph Output ["Multi-Stack Clean Architecture Scaffolding"]
        direction LR
        S_GO["Go Clean Architecture"]
        S_PY["Python FastAPI Hexagonal"]
        S_REACT["React 19 Feature-Based"]
        S_RUST["Rust Actix / Tokio"]
        S_NODE["Node.js TypeScript Clean"]
    end

    Agents <==>|"Tools / Skills / Instructions"| Engine
    Engine <==>|"Deterministic Hashing & Living State"| Specs
    Engine ==>|"Scaffolds Source Code & TDD Suites"| Output

    classDef agentBox fill:#faf5ff,stroke:#9333ea,stroke-width:1.5px,color:#3b0764,rx:6px,ry:6px;
    classDef engineBox fill:#f8fafc,stroke:#0284c7,stroke-width:1.5px,color:#0f172a,rx:6px,ry:6px;
    classDef specBox fill:#ffffff,stroke:#475569,stroke-width:1.5px,color:#1e293b,rx:6px,ry:6px;
    classDef outBox fill:#f0fdf4,stroke:#16a34a,stroke-width:1.5px,color:#14532d,rx:6px,ry:6px;
    
    class AG1,AG2,AG3,AG4,AG5,AG6 agentBox;
    class CLI,MCP,HASHER,LOCK,COMPACTOR,RULES,DRIFT engineBox;
    class FRONTMATTER,DAG,STATE,EVENTS,CONTEXT specBox;
    class S_GO,S_PY,S_REACT,S_RUST,S_NODE outBox;
```

---

## Quick Start

```bash
git clone https://github.com/seismael/genops.git my-project
cd my-project
```

Initialize across all coding agent platforms:

```bash
# Sync all agent instruction files (AGENTS.md, CLAUDE.md, Cursor rules, Copilot, Windsurf, Aider)
python .agents/scripts/genops.py init --agent all
```

Execute the pipeline:

```bash
/genops-prd            # Define product requirements (Principal PM)
/genops-hld            # Design system architecture (Principal Architect)
/genops-adr            # Document architectural decisions (Staff Engineer)
/genops-lld            # Specify low-level design & contracts (Lead Engineer)
/genops-code           # Scaffold Clean Architecture & TDD tests (Principal SWE)
```

---

## Pipeline Presets

Switch between specialized industry pipelines using presets:

```bash
# Software Specification Pipeline (Default)
python .agents/scripts/genops.py init --preset software-spec

# Systematic Research Pipeline (PRISMA, Hypotheses, Empirical Reports)
python .agents/scripts/genops.py init --preset research

# Strategic Design Pipeline (Design Briefs, Wireframes, Design Tokens, Prototypes)
python .agents/scripts/genops.py init --preset design
```

---

## Model Context Protocol (MCP) Integration

Any agent environment supporting MCP can expose GenOps tools directly:

```json
{
  "mcpServers": {
    "genops": {
      "command": "python",
      "args": [".agents/scripts/genops.py", "mcp"]
    }
  }
}
```

Exposed MCP tools:
- `genops_validate`: Validates configuration, presets, templates, and scaffolds.
- `genops_status`: Retrieves live pipeline health and staleness graph.
- `genops_hash`: Computes cross-platform LF-normalized SHA-256 hashes.
- `genops_record`: Atomically records stage state with lockfile safety and triggers memory compaction.
- `genops_compact`: Synthesizes living project memory into `.agents/context/CONTEXT.md`.
- `genops_verify`: Executes compiler and linter diagnostics across polyglot source workspace.
- `genops_scaffold`: Expands LLD module stubs and templates into `src/`.
- `genops_graph`: Generates specification lineage graph and DAG visualization.
- `genops_drift`: Runs CI/CD anti-drift check between LLD specs and source code.
- `genops_check_rules`: Enforces cross-layer semantic validation rules.
- `genops_rtm`: Generates bidirectional Requirements Traceability Matrix.
- `genops_context`: Slices the DAG for domain-targeted prompt loading.
- `genops_report`: Generates executive self-contained HTML dashboard.

---

## Clean Architecture Polyglot Scaffolds

`/genops-code` generates production-grade Clean / Hexagonal Architecture folders in `src/`:

```
src/{module}/
├── cmd/
│   └── main.go                    # Bootstrap & DI container
├── internal/
│   ├── domain/                    # Pure business logic & invariant validation
│   ├── ports/                     # Primary & Secondary interface definitions
│   ├── service/                   # Application use-case orchestrators
│   ├── adapters/                  # Secondary infrastructure (Postgres, Redis)
│   └── handlers/                  # Primary ingress adapters (REST, gRPC)
└── tests/
    └── contract/                  # OpenAPI / Contract test fixtures
```

Available built-in scaffolds (`.agents/scaffolds/`):
- `go-service`: Hexagonal Go microservice (Gin/pgx/testify).
- `python-fastapi`: Async Python Clean Service (FastAPI/Pydantic v2/SQLAlchemy).
- `react-vite`: React 19 SPA with feature-based architecture and Tailwind design tokens.
- `rust-service`: Asynchronous Rust microservice (Actix Web/Tokio/Serde).
- `node-service`: TypeScript microservice (Express/Zod/Vitest).
- `go-library`: Reusable Go domain package.

---

## Requirements

- Any AI coding agent (Claude Code, Cursor, Antigravity, GitHub Copilot, Windsurf, OpenCode, Aider)
- Python 3.8+ (standard library only, zero external pip dependencies)

=== FILE: TEMP.md ===
To elevate GenOps from a structured specification pipeline into an autonomous, enterprise-grade engineering engine, the framework must evolve from **passive questionnaire handlers** into **active cognitive architectural partners**.

The current GenOps architecture has established the foundation: deterministic LF-normalized SHA-256 state tracking, atomic locking, domain-split modular files, MCP tool-calling, and clean SoC boundaries.

Below is the architectural blueprint to transform GenOps skills, templates, context memory, and tooling into **Super Skills**.

---

```
                       EVOLUTION TO SUPER SKILLS
  Current Baseline (v2.0)               Super Skills (v3.0 Target)
┌─────────────────────────────┐       ┌─────────────────────────────┐
│ • Linear Q&A interviews     │       │ • Socratic Architectural    │
│ • Markdown stubs & DDL      │ ────► │   Challenger (Pushes back)  │
│ • Flat entity scaffolding   │       │ • Executable Contracts      │
│ • Human-only review gates   │       │   (OpenAPI, Protobuf, DDL)  │
│ • Static CONTEXT.md memory  │       │ • TDD & Compiler Feedback   │
└─────────────────────────────┘       │ • Active Memory Compaction  │
                                      └─────────────────────────────┘

```

---

## 1. Transforming Skills into "Super Skills" (`.agents/skills/`)

### A. The "Architectural Challenger" Mode (Socratic Probing)

* **The Current Gap:** Current stage skills ask template questions linearly (e.g., *"What architecture fits best? A) Monolith B) Microservices..."*) and record whatever the user types without pressure-testing assumptions.


* **The Super Skill Upgrade:** Convert the interview step into a **collaborative stress-test**. The agent should proactively challenge choices based on non-functional requirements (NFRs):
* *Example:* If the user specifies *Microservices* for a product targeting 10 requests/sec, the agent pushes back: *"Given the target scale, microservices introduce distributed transaction and networking overhead without clear ROI. Why not a Modular Monolith first?"*
* It presents concrete architectural trade-offs (Latency vs. Consistency, Cost vs. Throughput, CAP Theorem implications).



### B. Autonomous "Critic Pass" (Simulated Staff Review)

* **The Current Gap:** Step 6 (`VALIDATE`) currently checks cross-layer mapping rules (e.g., PRD user story $\to$ HLD component).


* **The Super Skill Upgrade:** Introduce an internal **Cognitive Critic Pass** before `PRESENT`:
* **Security Critic:** Evaluates the draft against STRIDE threat modeling (Authentication, Tampering, Repudiation, Info Disclosure, Denial of Service, Elevation of Privilege).
* **Resilience Critic:** Evaluates failure modes (What happens on database timeouts? How are retries and circuit breakers configured? Are idempotency keys used for webhook handlers?).
* **Observability Critic:** Verifies distributed tracing (OpenTelemetry span propagation), metrics (`p99` latency, error rates), and structured audit logging.



### C. Persona Specialization by Layer

Equip each skill with a tailored system prompt and cognitive lens:

* `/genops-prd`: **Principal Product Manager** (Focuses on user value, edge cases, unit economics, and scope boundaries).


* `/genops-hld`: **Principal Systems Architect** (Focuses on system topology, data flow, failure domains, and scalability).


* `/genops-adr`: **Staff Systems Engineer** (Focuses on technology benchmarks, operational complexity, and long-term maintenance debt).


* `/genops-lld`: **Lead Backend/Frontend Engineer** (Focuses on DDD aggregate roots, clean interfaces, and database indexes).


* `/genops-code`: **Principal Software Engineer** (Enforces TDD, strict typing, error propagation, and zero compiler warnings).



---

## 2. Upgrading Specification Templates (`.agents/templates/`)

```
                         SPECIFICATION TIERS
  Documents-as-Context (Markdown)  ──►  Executable Machine Specs (Contracts)
  ┌─────────────────────────────┐       ┌─────────────────────────────┐
  │ • Markdown ERDs             │       │ • Executable OpenAPI 3.1    │
  │ • Markdown Table Schemas    │       │ • Raw DDL / Migration SQL   │
  │ • Narrative API endpoints   │       │ • Protocol Buffers / gRPC   │
  └─────────────────────────────┘       └─────────────────────────────┘

```

### A. From Informational Markdown to Executable Contracts

* **The Current Gap:** LLD schemas and API contracts are documented primarily as Markdown tables and prose descriptions.


* **The Super Skill Upgrade:** Require templates to produce **executable contract artifacts**:
* **API Contracts:** Generate valid **OpenAPI 3.1 (YAML/JSON)** or **Protobuf (`.proto`)** files within `docs/contracts/`.
* **Database Schemas:** Generate concrete **SQL DDL migration scripts** (PostgreSQL / SQLite dialect) with indexes, foreign keys, and cascading rules.
* **Event Contracts:** Generate JSONSchema / CloudEvents specifications for message queues and event buses.



### B. Explicit Fault-Tolerance & Edge-Case Matrices

Upgrade `HLD-domain.md.template` and `LLD-domain.md.template` to include mandatory engineering tables:

* **Concurrency & Locking:** Specifying Optimistic vs. Pessimistic locking strategies and transaction isolation levels.
* **Rate Limiting & Shedding:** Specifying token bucket parameters, throttling responses, and degradation pathways.
* **Data Retention & Privacy:** Explicit GDPR/PII tagging, encryption-at-rest specifications, and retention pruning policies.

---

## 3. Upgrading Context & Memory (`CONTEXT.md` & State Engine)

### A. Active Living Memory & Topology Synthesis

* **The Current Gap:** `CONTEXT.md` is largely a static placeholder glossary populated manually or after individual stages.


* **The Super Skill Upgrade:** Implement **Active Context Compaction**:
* After each stage approval, `genops.py` should programmatically synthesize an up-to-date **System Topology & Constraint Card** directly into `CONTEXT.md`.


* Any agent starting a task instantly gets a high-density, ~500-token summary of all accepted tech choices, active databases, core domain models, and communication protocols without needing to read every historical document.



### B. Graph-Aware Selective DAG Context Slicing

* Extend the `cmd_context` feature into an automatic prompt optimizer.


* When working on a single domain (e.g., `billing`), the agent automatically traverses the `.genops-graph.json` DAG, collects upstream PRD $\to$ HLD $\to$ ADR $\to$ LLD documents for `billing`, prunes all unrelated domains, and injects a 70–90% compressed context payload into the LLM context window.



---

## 4. Upgrading the Engine & Scaffolding (`genops.py` & `.agents/scaffolds/`)

```mermaid
flowchart LR
    subgraph AutonomousLoop ["Autonomous Scaffolding & Verification Loop"]
        direction TB
        SCAFF["1. genops scaffold<br/>(Expand DDD directories & stubs)"]
        TEST["2. Generate Unit & Contract Tests<br/>(TDD First: RED state)"]
        COMP["3. Compiler & Linter Verification<br/>(go vet / tsc / cargo check / ruff)"]
        DRIFT["4. genops drift<br/>(Assert 100% Spec-to-Code Sync)"]
        
        SCAFF --> TEST --> COMP --> DRIFT
    end

```

### A. TDD-First Scaffolding Protocol

* **The Current Gap:** `/genops-code` creates directory structures and skeleton structs with empty bodies or basic IDs.


* **The Super Skill Upgrade:** Mandate a **Test-Driven Scaffolding** sequence:
1. Scaffold module interfaces and domain model types.


2. Auto-generate **failing unit and contract tests** directly derived from LLD API contracts and invariants.


3. Implement minimal operational stubs.


4. Ensure the test harness executes and compiles cleanly.



### B. Compiler-in-the-Loop Feedback (`genops_verify`)

* Introduce an automated feedback tool into the MCP server / CLI: `genops verify`.


* When `/genops-code` runs, the engine detects installed toolchains:
* **Go:** `go vet ./...` & `go test -run=^$`

* **TypeScript:** `tsc --noEmit` & `vitest run`

* **Python:** `ruff check .` & `pytest --collect-only`

* **Rust:** `cargo check`



* If compilation fails, the compiler diagnostics are fed directly back into the agent context, allowing it to self-heal code stubs automatically before presenting for final human approval.

### C. Domain-Driven Design (DDD) Scaffolds

Upgrade scaffolds from basic directory layouts to **Clean / Hexagonal Architecture**:

* **Domain Layer:** Pure business entities, value objects, domain events, domain errors (zero framework dependencies).


* **Application Layer:** Use cases, service interfaces, input/output DTOs.
* **Infrastructure Layer:** Concrete database adapters (Postgres, Redis), HTTP handlers (Gin, FastAPI, Actix), third-party API clients.



---

## 5. Strategic Comparison & Implementation Matrix

| Capability | Current GenOps v2.0 | Super Skills Target (v3.0) | Value Added |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Interview Logic** | Static question list

 | Socratic probing & architectural challenge | Prevents architectural mistakes and premature complexity |
| **Review Process** | Human approval gate (`<HARD-GATE>`)

 | Dual-pass: Automated Staff Critic + Human Gate | Catches security vulnerabilities and failure modes early |
| **Design Artifacts** | Markdown descriptions & tables

 | OpenAPI 3.1, Protobuf, and raw SQL DDL | Machine-executable contracts ready for code generation |
| **Scaffolding Depth** | Empty structs with `ID string`<br> | DDD Domain Models, Value Objects, & Interfaces | High-fidelity, production-ready project baselines |
| **Verification** | Markdown regex anti-drift

 | Compiler toolchain checks (`tsc`, `cargo`, `go`) | Guarantees code builds with zero compile errors |
| **Context Memory** | Static `CONTEXT.md`<br> | Active auto-compacting topology memory | Prevents agent context decay across long sessions |

## Phase 1: Upgrading the Foundation — `genops-stage` Protocol

The universal cognitive engine for every GenOps skill is **`genops-stage`**. Upgrading this core protocol automatically elevates all 17 skills across every pipeline preset.

---

### Key Super Skill Enhancements

1. **The Socratic Challenger (Step 4 — INTERVIEW):** Shifts the agent from a passive questionnaire recorder into an active architectural sparring partner. The agent must challenge premature complexity, probe scale/NFR mismatches, and present trade-offs before accepting user answers.


2. **Autonomous Multi-Perspective Critic Pass (Step 6 — VALIDATE):** Before presenting any artifact, the agent performs an internal simulated Staff Review across three vectors:
* **Security:** STRIDE threat analysis, auth boundaries, input validation.


* **Resilience:** Failure modes, timeouts, retry storms, circuit breaking, idempotency.


* **Observability:** Distributed tracing, metrics, structured audit logs.




3. **Active Memory Compaction (Step 9 — RECORD):** Automatically distills and syncs new domain models, technology decisions, and non-negotiables into `.agents/context/CONTEXT.md`, preventing context window degradation across long sessions.



---

### Upgraded File: `.agents/skills/genops-stage/SKILL.md`

```markdown
---
name: genops-stage
description: Use when executing any GenOps pipeline stage. Universal cognitive protocol for all GenOps stage skills.
---

# GenOps Super Stage Protocol (v3.0)

Template-driven cognitive protocol: PRE-FLIGHT → LOAD → DOMAINS → CHECK → INTERVIEW (Socratic) → GENERATE → VALIDATE (Critic Pass) → PRESENT → APPROVE → RECORD (Compact) → TRANSITION.

Hot-path protocol enforcing architectural integrity, executable specifications, and deterministic state tracking.

<HARD-GATE>
Do NOT skip APPROVE. Do NOT transition without explicit human approval unless --flow or --nonstop. "It looks fine" is NOT approval.
</HARD-GATE>

## 0. PRE-FLIGHT — Dependency & Health Verification
Halt on first failure. Run `python .agents/scripts/genops.py validate` (or MCP `genops_validate`) if available.
- **Init:** AGENTS.md has `<!-- GENOPS:START -->`. `genops.yaml` exists.
- **Stage:** Current stage `id` defined in `genops.yaml`. Output directory exists.
- **Template:** `.agents/templates/<template>` exists with `## Interview` and `## Output` sections.
- **State:** `docs/.genops-state.json` exists (v2.0 schema).
- **Upstream:** Every path in `requires` exists with ≥1 approved artifact.

## 1. LOAD — Context Assembly
Read stage config from `genops.yaml`. Load all upstream documents from `requires` paths. Read stage template. Load `.agents/context/CONTEXT.md` for active system topology, glossary, and architectural constraints.

## 2. DOMAINS — Scope & Naming
Enforce domain-split structure: `{STAGE}-{NNN}-{slug}.md`. Target single domain if `--domain <slug>` is specified; otherwise discover all domains from upstream. Auto-increment sequence prefix `NNN`.

## 3. CHECK — Cryptographic Staleness
Compute live LF-normalized SHA-256 hash of all `requires` paths (`genops hash <path>`). Compare to recorded `requires_hash`. If identical and state is `approved`, prompt: "Stage already complete and consistent. Re-run?"

## 4. INTERVIEW — Socratic Architectural Challenger
Ask template questions ONE at a time. Do NOT passively record answers:
- **Challenge Premature Complexity:** If target scale/traffic is low, challenge distributed microservices or complex event buses; suggest simpler modular monoliths.
- **Probe Failure Modes:** Demand explicit strategies for latency, network partitions, and data loss.
- **Surface Trade-offs:** Present concrete trade-offs (e.g., Consistency vs. Availability, Latency vs. Throughput, Operational Cost).

## 5. GENERATE — Executable Specification
Generate `<outputs>/<STAGE>-<NNN>-<slug}.md` adhering strictly to template structure with standardized YAML frontmatter (`id`, `domain`, `stage`, `version`, `status`, `upstream_refs`, `downstream_refs`, `tags`).
- Placeholders (`TODO`, `TBD`) are strictly prohibited.
- Technical specs must include concrete schemas, DDL, OpenAPI/contracts, or Mermaid diagrams.
- For `code` stage: execute `python .agents/scripts/genops.py scaffold`.

## 6. VALIDATE — Multi-Perspective Critic Pass
Run declarative `validation_rules` from `genops.yaml` and execute an internal Staff Review:
1. **Security Critic:** Assess STRIDE vectors, auth boundaries, input validation, and secret handling.
2. **Resilience Critic:** Check timeout policies, retry backoffs, circuit breakers, and idempotency keys.
3. **Observability Critic:** Verify OpenTelemetry tracing spans, health metrics, and audit logging.
4. **Consistency:** Verify all upstream "Needs ADR" items are resolved and mapped downstream.
If critical gaps are found, surface recommendations immediately before presentation.

## 7. PRESENT — Preview & Impact Analysis
Display executive summary of generated artifacts, resolved decisions, and downstream impact map (highlighting all downstream files that will become `stale`).

## 8. APPROVE — Human Hard Gate
Require explicit human confirmation ("approved", "proceed", "yes"). If changes are requested, apply delta modifications only and re-run Step 6 (VALIDATE).

## 9. RECORD — Atomic State & Active Memory Compaction
Execute `python .agents/scripts/genops.py record <stage-id> --actor <user|agent>`.
- Updates `docs/.genops-state.json` and appends immutable event to `docs/.genops-events.jsonl`.
- **Memory Compaction:** Extract new domain models, accepted technologies, and constraints; compact and update `.agents/context/CONTEXT.md`.

## 10. TRANSITION — Handoff
- `--nonstop`: Automatically invoke next stage with `--nonstop`.
- `--flow`: Automatically invoke next stage (SoC default).
- Default: "Stage approved. Run /genops-<next> now or save for later?"
- Terminal (`next: []`): "Pipeline complete. Scaffolding ready in src/."

```


## Phase 2: Upgrading Stage 1 — Product Requirements (`PRD`)

In this phase, we elevate **`/genops-prd`** and its corresponding template from basic feature lists into rigorous, measurable product requirements.

### Key Super Skill Enhancements for Stage 1

1. **Principal Product Manager Persona:** The agent actively probes for quantifiable business value, concrete North Star metrics, and strict return-on-investment (ROI) justifications rather than accepting generic wishlists.
2. **Behavior-Driven Development (BDD) Criteria:** Enforces the `Given-When-Then` format for user story acceptance criteria, eliminating ambiguity before design begins.
3. **Explicit Anti-Features (Scope Hardening):** Demands a defined list of capabilities that the system will *explicitly not* build in this iteration to prevent scope creep.
4. **Context Seed Ingestion:** Automatically extracts domain entities, actor roles, and non-negotiable compliance rules to seed `.agents/context/CONTEXT.md`.



---

### 1. Upgraded Skill: `.agents/skills/genops-prd/SKILL.md`

```markdown
---
name: genops-prd
description: Use when creating product requirements, defining user stories, establishing success metrics, or scoping a new product/feature. First stage in the GenOps pipeline.
---

# Product Requirements (PRD) — Principal PM Persona

First stage. No upstream dependencies. Generates specification documents into `docs/prd/`.

**Cognitive Role:** Principal Product Manager. Socratic, value-driven, mathematically precise, and protective against scope creep.

**Config:** `id: prd`, `requires: []`, `outputs: docs/prd/`, `file_pattern: PRD-{NNN}-{slug}.md`, `template: prd/PRD-domain.md.template`, `next: [hld]`

## Execution Protocol

1. **PRE-FLIGHT**
   - Verify `genops.yaml` contains stage `prd` with valid configuration[cite: 4].
   - Verify `.agents/templates/prd/PRD-domain.md.template` exists with `## Interview` and `## Output` sections[cite: 4].
   - Verify `docs/prd/` directory exists[cite: 4].

2. **LOAD**
   - Read template and extract Socratic interview questions and document output structure[cite: 4].
   - Load `.agents/context/CONTEXT.md` to check for pre-existing constraints or domain terms[cite: 4].

3. **DOMAINS**
   - Inquire: "Single product domain or multi-domain breakdown (e.g., catalog, checkout, billing)?"[cite: 4]
   - Generate one PRD per domain: `PRD-{NNN}-{slug}.md`[cite: 4].

4. **CHECK**
   - First stage: verify if `docs/prd/PRD-*.md` files already exist[cite: 4]. Prompt if re-running.

5. **INTERVIEW (Socratic Principal PM)**
   - Ask template interview questions ONE at a time[cite: 4].
   - **Challenge Vague Objectives:** Demand measurable baselines and targets (e.g., "Reduce checkout latency from 800ms to <200ms" instead of "Make it faster").
   - **Enforce BDD Acceptance:** Ensure each user story has at least one concrete `Given-When-Then` scenario.
   - **Lock Scope Boundaries:** Challenge any feature without clear ROI and relegate it to "Out of Scope / Anti-Features".

6. **GENERATE**
   - Generate `docs/prd/PRD-{NNN}-{slug}.md` using the template output structure[cite: 4].
   - Include standard YAML frontmatter (`id`, `domain`, `stage: prd`, `version: 1.0.0`, `status: draft`, `upstream_refs: []`, `downstream_refs: []`, `tags`)[cite: 4].
   - Prohibit placeholder text (`TODO`, `TBD`, `N/A`)[cite: 4].

7. **VALIDATE (Critic Pass)**
   - Verify all user stories have measurable acceptance criteria[cite: 4].
   - Verify that all core personas have defined constraints and permissions.
   - Verify that non-functional requirements (NFRs) specify numeric bounds (latency, throughput, availability).

8. **PRESENT → APPROVE**
   - Present executive summary, core capabilities, and scope boundaries[cite: 4].
   - Enforce hard confirmation gate (`<HARD-GATE>`)[cite: 4].

9. **RECORD & COMPACT**
   - Run `python .agents/scripts/genops.py record prd --actor user`[cite: 4].
   - Extract domain personas, business terms, and non-negotiables into `.agents/context/CONTEXT.md`[cite: 4].

10. **TRANSITION**
    - Transition to `/genops-hld` based on active flow mode (`--nonstop`, `--flow`, or default prompt)[cite: 4].

<HARD-GATE>
Do NOT proceed to /genops-hld without explicit user approval at Step 8[cite: 4].
</HARD-GATE>

```

---

### 2. Upgraded Template: `.agents/templates/prd/PRD-domain.md.template`

# {Domain} — Product Requirements

## Interview

Questions asked ONE at a time. The agent operates as a Principal Product Manager, challenging assumptions and demanding numeric precision.

**Required:**
1. **Problem & North Star** — "What specific customer friction or business bottleneck are we solving? What is the single measurable North Star metric that defines success (including current baseline vs. target)?"
2. **Target Personas & Access Boundaries** — "Who are the primary user personas interacting with this domain? What are their operational goals, permission levels, and primary constraints?"
3. **Core User Stories (BDD Format)** — "What are the 3-5 critical capabilities required? For each capability, formulate as: As a <role>, I want <feature> so that <benefit>, with acceptance criteria in Given-When-Then format."
4. **Scope Boundaries & Anti-Features** — "What is strictly IN-SCOPE for v1, and what are the explicit ANTI-FEATURES (items deliberately excluded to prevent scope creep)?"

**Optional / Operational:**
5. **Scale, Latency & SLOs** — "What are the expected traffic baselines? (e.g., peak requests/sec, concurrent users, acceptable p99 latency threshold)?"
6. **Compliance, Privacy & Security Constraints** — "Are there strict regulatory requirements (GDPR, SOC2, HIPAA, PCI-DSS) or data sovereignty constraints governing this domain?"

|---|---|---|---|

## 4. Key Capabilities & User Stories (BDD Format)

* **Story:** As an `{Actor}`, I want `{Action}` so that `{Business Outcome}`.
* **Priority:** `P1` <!-- P1 (Must Have), P2 (Should Have), P3 (Nice to Have) -->

  * **Scenario 1 (Happy Path):**

|---|---|---|

| **Throughput** | Sustained load capability | $\ge 500\text{ req/sec}$ peak |
| **Availability** | Multi-region uptime target | $99.95\%$ per calendar month |
| **Data Retention** | Audit and event log lifecycle | 365 days encrypted retention |

## 7. Assumptions, Dependencies & Risks

### Strategic Assumptions
* <!-- Key business and technical assumptions -->

### System Dependencies
* <!-- Upstream systems, external APIs, third-party authentication -->

### Risk & Mitigation Matrix (STRIDE & Operational)
| Risk Description | Severity | Likelihood | Mitigation Strategy |
|---|---|---|---|
| High API traffic spikes during event windows | High | Med | Implement distributed token-bucket rate limiting and circuit breaking |
| Unauthorized access to sensitive tenant records | Critical | Low | Enforce mandatory ABAC / RBAC checks at the API gateway tier |

## Phase 3: Upgrading Stage 2 — High-Level Design (`HLD`)

In this phase, we elevate **`/genops-hld`** and its corresponding template into a rigorous architectural blueprint authored under the **Principal Systems Architect** persona.

---

### Key Super Skill Enhancements for Stage 2

1. **Principal Systems Architect Persona:** The agent challenges architectural patterns (Monolith vs. Microservices vs. Event-Driven), identifies single points of failure (SPOFs), evaluates CAP theorem trade-offs, and enforces domain boundaries.


2. **C4 Topology Modeling:** Upgrades system diagrams to formal C4 Context and Container levels using Mermaid notation.


3. **Explicit Failure Domains & Resiliency:** Mandates concrete resilience patterns (circuit breakers, timeouts, retry budgets, dead-letter queues, rate limiting, and bulkhead isolation).


4. **Automated "Needs ADR" Discovery:** Programmatically scans for unresolved architectural, storage, or protocol trade-offs and generates a structured catalog of items that must become ADRs in Stage 3.


5. **Cross-Layer Mapping Verification:** Validates that 100% of the Key Capabilities from `PRD` map directly to at least one component or sequence flow.



---

### 1. Upgraded Skill: `.agents/skills/genops-hld/SKILL.md`

```markdown
---
name: genops-hld
description: Use when designing system topology, component boundaries, data flow, and integration points. Second stage in the GenOps pipeline. Requires completed PRD.
---

# High-Level Design (HLD) — Principal Systems Architect Persona

Second stage. Requires an approved PRD. Generates system design specifications into `docs/hld/`.

**Cognitive Role:** Principal Systems Architect. Systems thinker, failure-domain obsessed, rigorous with boundaries, protocols, and CAP theorem trade-offs.

**Config:** `id: hld`, `requires: [docs/prd/]`, `outputs: docs/hld/`, `file_pattern: HLD-{NNN}-{slug}.md`, `template: hld/HLD-domain.md.template`, `next: [adr]`

## Execution Protocol

1. **PRE-FLIGHT**
   - Verify `docs/prd/` exists and contains ≥1 approved PRD document[cite: 4].
   - Verify `.agents/templates/hld/HLD-domain.md.template` exists with `## Interview` and `## Output` sections[cite: 4].
   - Run `python .agents/scripts/genops.py validate`[cite: 4].

2. **LOAD**
   - Read all upstream `docs/prd/*.md` documents[cite: 4].
   - Read `.agents/context/CONTEXT.md` for existing constraints, glossaries, and technology preferences[cite: 4].
   - Read `HLD-domain.md.template` to extract interview questions and output structure[cite: 4].

3. **DOMAINS**
   - Discover domain slugs from upstream PRD files[cite: 4].
   - If `--domain <slug>` is specified, scope execution strictly to that domain[cite: 4].
   - Assign file naming: `docs/hld/HLD-{NNN}-{slug}.md`[cite: 4].

4. **CHECK**
   - Compute LF-normalized hash of `docs/prd/` via `python .agents/scripts/genops.py hash docs/prd/`[cite: 4].
   - Compare with stored `requires_hash` in `docs/.genops-state.json`[cite: 4]. If state is `approved` and unchanged, prompt before re-generating[cite: 4].

5. **INTERVIEW (Socratic Principal Architect)**
   - Ask template interview questions ONE at a time[cite: 4].
   - **Challenge Distributed Complexity:** Interrogate microservices choices against throughput and team size. If a modular monolith suffices, advocate for it.
   - **Enforce Resiliency:** Demand explicit failure recovery mechanisms for every integration point (e.g., fallback behaviors, message queues, circuit breakers).
   - **Identify "Needs ADR" Items:** Whenever an unvetted technology, database, message queue, or protocol choice arises, flag it immediately for the Architecture Decisions stage[cite: 4].

6. **GENERATE**
   - Generate `docs/hld/HLD-{NNN}-{slug}.md` using the template output structure[cite: 4].
   - Include standard YAML frontmatter (`id`, `domain`, `stage: hld`, `version: 1.0.0`, `status: draft`, `upstream_refs: ["PRD-NNN-slug"]`, `downstream_refs: []`, `tags`)[cite: 4].
   - Include formal Mermaid `C4Context` and `sequenceDiagram` definitions[cite: 4].

7. **VALIDATE (Critic Pass & Cross-Layer Rules)**
   - **Coverage Check:** Verify that 100% of PRD Key Capabilities map to at least one component or sequence flow[cite: 4].
   - **Resilience Critic:** Ensure every synchronous cross-boundary call has a defined timeout, retry policy, and fallback.
   - **Security Critic:** Ensure authentication/authorization enforcement points and data transit boundaries are specified.
   - **ADR Backlog:** Ensure all technology choices without prior consensus are cataloged in the "Technology Decisions (Needs ADR)" section[cite: 4].

8. **PRESENT → APPROVE**
   - Present the system topology diagram, component boundary summary, and the list of flagged "Needs ADR" items[cite: 4].
   - Enforce hard confirmation gate (`<HARD-GATE>`)[cite: 4].

9. **RECORD & COMPACT**
   - Run `python .agents/scripts/genops.py record hld --actor user`[cite: 4].
   - Update `.agents/context/CONTEXT.md` with newly defined component boundaries and communication protocols[cite: 4].

10. **TRANSITION**
    - Transition to `/genops-adr` based on active flow mode (`--nonstop`, `--flow`, or default prompt)[cite: 4].

<HARD-GATE>
Do NOT proceed to /genops-adr without explicit human approval at Step 8[cite: 4].
</HARD-GATE>

```

### 2. Upgraded Template: `.agents/templates/hld/HLD-domain.md.template`

```markdown
# {Domain} — High-Level Design

## Interview

Questions asked ONE at a time. The agent operates as a Principal Systems Architect, enforcing structural decoupling, clean interfaces, and resilient distributed patterns.

**Required:**
1. **System Topology & Architecture Pattern** — "What macro-architecture fits best (Modular Monolith, Event-Driven Microservices, Serverless, CQRS/Event Sourcing)? What is the primary justification against simpler alternatives?"
2. **Component Boundaries & Core Responsibilities** — "What are the 3-5 primary components or services in this domain? For each: what is its single responsibility, what public interface does it expose, and what are its direct dependencies?"
3. **End-to-End Critical Data Flows** — "Walk through the primary data flow and transaction path end-to-end. Which components interact synchronously (REST/gRPC) vs. asynchronously (Events/Queues)?"
4. **Failure Domains & Resilience Strategy** — "What happens when a downstream dependency or database fails? What are the explicit timeout, retry, circuit breaker, and fallback strategies?"

**Optional / Operational:**
5. **Data Storage & Consistency Model** — "What data consistency guarantees are required (Strict ACID, Eventual Consistency, Read-after-Write)? What storage paradigm fits (Relational, Document, Key-Value, Vector)?"
6. **Cross-Cutting Concerns** — "What is the ingress/gateway strategy, authentication propagation model, and distributed tracing approach?"
7. **Technology Candidates (Needs ADR)** — "What technologies, databases, frameworks, or external infrastructure are being considered that require an explicit Architecture Decision Record (ADR)?"

created_at: {TIMESTAMP}
updated_at: {TIMESTAMP}

# {Domain} — High-Level Design Specification

## 1. System Overview & Architectural Approach

## 2. System Topology (C4 Context & Container)

  title System Context Diagram — {Domain}

  System(domainSystem, "{Domain} System", "Handles core domain logic and workflows")
  System_Ext(authProvider, "Identity Provider", "Validates JWT tokens & RBAC")
  System_Ext(eventBus, "Message Broker / Event Bus", "Asynchronous domain event streaming")
  
  Rel(user, domainSystem, "Invokes APIs", "HTTPS / JSON")

## 4. End-to-End Data Flow & Sequence

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    actor Client as Client / Caller
    participant GW as API Gateway
    participant Svc as {Domain} Service
    participant DB as Persistence Store
    participant Broker as Event Broker

    Client->>GW: POST /api/v1/{domain}/action (Bearer Token)
    GW->>GW: Validate JWT & Rate Limits
    GW->>Svc: Forward sanitized request
    Svc->>DB: Begin Transaction & Query State
    alt State Valid
        Svc->>DB: Commit State Change
        Svc-->>Broker: Emit {Domain}CreatedEvent (Async)
        Svc-->>GW: 201 Created (Payload)
        GW-->>Client: 201 Created
    else Validation or Concurrency Conflict
        Svc->>DB: Rollback
        Svc-->>GW: 409 Conflict / 422 Unprocessable
        GW-->>Client: Structured Error Response
    end

```

## 5. Failure Domains & Resiliency Framework

| Integration Point | Failure Mode | Resilience Pattern | Fallback / Recovery Strategy |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Primary Database | Connection Timeout / Pool Exhaustion | Exponential Backoff with Jitter | Return 503 with `Retry-After` header; alert APM |
| Downstream Sync API | Network Partition / High Latency | Circuit Breaker (50% fail in 10s) | Serve degraded cached response or enqueue task |
| Event Broker | Broker Unreachable on Publish | Outbox Pattern (Transactional Store) | Relay worker drains outbox once broker recovers |

## 6. Integration Points & Protocols

| Integration Name | Protocol | Direction | Sync / Async | Payload Format | SLA Target |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Ingress API | HTTPS | Inbound | Synchronous | JSON / Protobuf | $p99 < 150\text{ms}$ |
| Domain Events | Kafka / AMQP | Outbound | Asynchronous | CloudEvents (JSON) | $< 50\text{ms}$ ack |
| Database | TCP (Postgres Wire) | Outbound | Synchronous | Binary SQL | $p99 < 10\text{ms}$ |

## 7. Non-Functional Requirements (NFR) Allocations

* **Throughput & Scalability:** Support $\ge 1,000\text{ req/sec}$ horizontal scale with stateless compute nodes.
* **Data Consistency:** Strict serializability for financial/inventory writes; eventual consistency ($< 500\text{ms}$) for analytics read replicas.
* **Security & Auth:** TLS 1.3 in transit; mTLS between internal microservices; AES-256-GCM at rest.

## 8. Technology Decisions Backlog (Needs ADR)

| Area | Decision Item | Recommended Candidate | Alternatives to Evaluate | Status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Runtime / Language | Programming Language & Engine | Go 1.22+ | Rust, TypeScript (Node), Python | Needs ADR |
| Persistence | Primary Datastore | PostgreSQL 16 | MySQL, MongoDB, DynamoDB | Needs ADR |
| Transport Protocol | Inter-Service Communication | gRPC / HTTP2 | REST (HTTP/1.1), GraphQL | Needs ADR |

```


## Phase 4: Upgrading Stage 3 — Architecture Decision Records (`ADR`)

In this phase, we elevate **`/genops-adr`** and its corresponding template into a rigorous trade-off evaluation engine authored under the **Staff Systems Engineer** persona.

---

### Key Super Skill Enhancements for Stage 3

1. **Staff Systems Engineer Persona:** Operates with a deep focus on Total Cost of Ownership (TCO), operational complexity, team velocity, long-term technical debt, and production maintainability.
2. **Weighted Decision Matrix:** Replaces subjective pros/cons with a multi-criteria scoring matrix (Performance, Developer Ergonomics, Operational Overhead, Ecosystem Maturity, and Failure Characteristics).
3. **Downstream Directives for LLD & Code:** Explicitly defines the concrete constraints and invariants that the accepted decision imposes on database schemas, API contracts, domain entities, and code scaffolds.


4. **Rollback & Migration Strategy:** Requires every major architectural choice to define an exit strategy (e.g., abstraction boundaries, feature flags, or migration paths if the chosen technology needs to be swapped).
5. **Interactive "Needs ADR" Resolution:** Scans upstream `docs/hld/*.md` files for items cataloged under "Technology Decisions (Needs ADR)" and systematically resolves them one by one.



---

### 1. Upgraded Skill: `.agents/skills/genops-adr/SKILL.md`

```markdown
---
name: genops-adr
description: Use when making architectural decisions with trade-off analysis, technology selection, and design pattern choices. Third stage in the GenOps pipeline. Requires completed HLD.
---

# Architecture Decision Records (ADR) — Staff Systems Engineer Persona

Third stage. Requires an approved HLD[cite: 4]. Generates modular decision records into `docs/architecture/`[cite: 4].

**Cognitive Role:** Staff Systems Engineer. Pragmatic, operational-debt averse, rigorous evaluator of trade-offs, and defender of long-term maintainability.

**Config:** `id: adr`, `requires: [docs/hld/]`, `outputs: docs/architecture/`, `file_pattern: ADR-{NNN}-{slug}.md`, `template: adr/ADR.md.template`, `next: [lld]`[cite: 4]

## Execution Protocol

1. **PRE-FLIGHT**
   - Verify `docs/hld/` exists with ≥1 approved HLD document[cite: 4].
   - Scan upstream HLDs for the `## 8. Technology Decisions Backlog (Needs ADR)` section to compile unresolved decision topics[cite: 4].
   - Verify `.agents/templates/adr/ADR.md.template` exists with `## Interview` and `## Output` sections[cite: 4].
   - Run `python .agents/scripts/genops.py validate`[cite: 4].

2. **LOAD**
   - Read all upstream `docs/hld/*.md` specifications[cite: 4].
   - Read `.agents/context/CONTEXT.md` for existing Technology Preferences and Architecture Constraints[cite: 4].
   - Read `ADR.md.template` to extract interview questions and output structure[cite: 4].

3. **DOMAINS & QUEUE**
   - Identify all unresolved "Needs ADR" items from HLD[cite: 4].
   - Iterate **ONE decision at a time**[cite: 4].
   - Assign file naming: `docs/architecture/ADR-{NNN}-{slug}.md`[cite: 4].

4. **CHECK**
   - Compute live LF-normalized hash of `docs/hld/` via `python .agents/scripts/genops.py hash docs/hld/`[cite: 4].
   - Verify that upstream HLD has not changed since last state record[cite: 4].

5. **INTERVIEW (Socratic Staff Engineer)**
   - Ask template interview questions ONE at a time[cite: 4].
   - **Challenge Resume-Driven Development:** Push back against bleeding-edge technologies or over-engineered frameworks when boring, battle-tested solutions deliver equal business value.
   - **Score Multi-Criteria Matrix:** Guide the user through scoring each viable alternative across 5 vectors (Performance, Simplicity, Operational Complexity, Ecosystem, and Cost).
   - **Define Concrete Downstream Constraints:** Extract exact interface boundaries and rules that LLD and Code must follow[cite: 4].

6. **GENERATE**
   - Generate `docs/architecture/ADR-{NNN}-{slug}.md` using the template output structure[cite: 4].
   - Include standard YAML frontmatter (`id`, `domain`, `stage: adr`, `version: 1.0.0`, `status: accepted`, `upstream_refs: ["HLD-NNN-slug"]`, `downstream_refs: []`, `tags`)[cite: 4].

7. **VALIDATE (Critic Pass & Cross-Layer Rules)**
   - **Rule Verification:** Check if any remaining "Needs ADR" items in HLD are unaddressed[cite: 4].
   - **Deprecation Integrity:** If this ADR supersedes a prior ADR, verify that the prior ADR status is updated to `superseded`[cite: 4].
   - **Contract Clarity:** Ensure explicit directives for LLD (e.g., driver versions, schema types, concurrency controls) are unambiguous[cite: 4].

8. **PRESENT → APPROVE**
   - Present executive summary of the decision, weighted scoring matrix, consequences, and downstream constraints[cite: 4].
   - Solicit feedback: "Approve this ADR, modify it, or evaluate an additional alternative?"[cite: 4]
   - After approval, ask: "Resolve next 'Needs ADR' item from HLD or proceed to /genops-lld?"[cite: 4]

9. **RECORD & COMPACT**
   - Run `python .agents/scripts/genops.py record adr --actor user`[cite: 4].
   - Update the **Technology Preferences** and **Architecture Constraints** tables in `.agents/context/CONTEXT.md` with the new decision and rationale[cite: 4].

10. **TRANSITION**
    - Once all critical ADRs are accepted, transition to `/genops-lld` based on active flow mode (`--nonstop`, `--flow`, or default prompt)[cite: 4].

<HARD-GATE>
Do NOT proceed to /genops-lld without explicit approval of all required architectural decisions[cite: 4].
</HARD-GATE>

```

## Phase 5: Upgrading Stage 4 — Low-Level Design (`LLD`)

In this phase, we elevate **`/genops-lld`** and its corresponding template into a precise, machine-executable detailed design specification authored under the **Lead Systems Engineer / Technical Lead** persona.

---

### Key Super Skill Enhancements for Stage 4

1. **Lead Systems Engineer Persona:** The agent focuses on Domain-Driven Design (DDD) aggregate boundaries, database indexing strategies, type safety, API contract compatibility, and error propagation semantics.
2. **Executable Machine Contracts:** Replaces narrative API descriptions and generic markdown tables with **OpenAPI 3.1 / gRPC Protobuf definitions** and **raw SQL DDL migration scripts** (with explicit primary keys, foreign keys, constraints, and indexes).


3. **Domain-Driven Design (DDD) Formalism:** Decomposes the domain into Aggregate Roots, Entities, Value Objects, Domain Events, and Repository interfaces.
4. **Structured Error Taxonomy:** Mandates an explicit error matrix mapping domain errors to HTTP status codes, gRPC status codes, error identifiers, and retryability flags.
5. **Standardized Project Structure Table:** Generates a structured `## Project Structure` section and `### Modules` table that `/genops-code` and `genops.py scaffold` consume directly to generate project code.


6. **Cross-Layer ADR Compliance Gate:** Verifies that 100% of the constraints defined in Stage 3 ADRs (e.g., driver versions, transaction isolation, interface boundaries) are enforced in schemas and contracts.



---

### 1. Upgraded Skill: `.agents/skills/genops-lld/SKILL.md`

```markdown
---
name: genops-lld
description: Use when defining class diagrams, database schemas, API contracts, and module interfaces. Fourth stage in the GenOps pipeline. Requires completed HLD and ADRs.
---

# Low-Level Design (LLD) — Lead Systems Engineer Persona

Fourth stage. Requires approved HLD and ADR specifications[cite: 4]. Generates detailed design contracts into `docs/lld/`[cite: 4].

**Config:** `id: lld`, `requires: [docs/hld/, docs/architecture/]`, `outputs: docs/lld/`, `file_pattern: LLD-{NNN}-{slug}.md`, `template: lld/LLD-domain.md.template`, `next: [code]`[cite: 4]

## Execution Protocol

1. **PRE-FLIGHT**
   - Verify `docs/hld/` and `docs/architecture/` exist and contain approved specifications[cite: 4].
   - Scan upstream ADRs to compile mandatory constraints (e.g., datastore choices, interface rules, libraries)[cite: 4].
   - Verify `.agents/templates/lld/LLD-domain.md.template` exists with `## Interview` and `## Output` sections[cite: 4].
   - Run `python .agents/scripts/genops.py validate`[cite: 4].

2. **LOAD**
   - Read all upstream `docs/hld/*.md` and `docs/architecture/*.md` documents[cite: 4].
   - Read `.agents/context/CONTEXT.md` for active technology preferences and architectural constraints[cite: 4].
   - Read `LLD-domain.md.template` to extract interview questions and output structure[cite: 4].

3. **DOMAINS**
   - Discover domain slugs from upstream HLD specifications[cite: 4].
   - If `--domain <slug>` is specified, scope execution strictly to that domain[cite: 4].

   - Compute live LF-normalized hashes of `docs/hld/` and `docs/architecture/` via `genops hash`[cite: 4].
   - Verify upstream dependencies are consistent and approved[cite: 4].

   - Start by confirming accepted ADR directives: *"ADR-001 mandates X, ADR-002 mandates Y. Confirming these directives govern this design."*[cite: 4]
   - Ask template interview questions ONE at a time[cite: 4].
   - **Enforce DDD Integrity:** Distinguish between Aggregate Roots, Entities, and immutable Value Objects. Reject bloated structs without clear invariants.
   - **Demand SQL / Schema Precision:** Demand real SQL DDL with column data types, foreign keys, cascade rules, and query-optimized indexes.
   - **Lock API Contracts:** Verify request validation rules, headers, authentication scopes, and structured error schemas.
   - **Select Scaffolds:** Confirm target module directories and scaffolds (`go-service`, `python-fastapi`, `react-vite`, `rust-service`, `node-service`, `go-library`) for Stage 5[cite: 4].

6. **GENERATE**
   - Generate `docs/lld/LLD-{NNN}-{slug}.md` using the template output structure[cite: 4].
   - Include standard YAML frontmatter (`id`, `domain`, `stage: lld`, `version: 1.0.0`, `status: draft`, `upstream_refs: ["HLD-NNN-slug", "ADR-NNN-slug"]`, `downstream_refs: []`, `tags`)[cite: 4].
   - Populate executable schemas, SQL DDL migrations, OpenAPI 3.1 specifications, and the `### Modules` scaffolding table[cite: 4].

7. **VALIDATE (Critic Pass & Cross-Layer Rules)**
   - **ADR Compliance:** Assert that every decision in accepted ADRs is implemented in the schema, interface signatures, or contracts[cite: 4].
   - **Index Optimality:** Ensure all foreign key columns and common query filter combinations have explicit indexes.
   - **Scaffold Compatibility:** Verify that every scaffold identifier in the `### Modules` table exists in `.agents/scaffolds/`[cite: 4].
   - **No Ambiguity:** Ensure zero placeholder types (`any`, `interface{}`, `TODO`) exist in entity and contract definitions[cite: 4].

8. **PRESENT → APPROVE**
   - Present entity model summary, schema DDL preview, API endpoint list, and the target module scaffolding table[cite: 4].
   - Enforce hard confirmation gate (`<HARD-GATE>`)[cite: 4].

9. **RECORD & COMPACT**
   - Run `python .agents/scripts/genops.py record lld --actor user`[cite: 4].
   - Update `.agents/context/CONTEXT.md` with new entity names, database table names, and API route definitions[cite: 4].

10. **TRANSITION**
    - Transition to `/genops-code` based on active flow mode (`--nonstop`, `--flow`, or default prompt)[cite: 4].

<HARD-GATE>
Do NOT proceed to /genops-code without explicit human approval at Step 8[cite: 4].
</HARD-GATE>

### 2. Upgraded Template: `.agents/templates/lld/LLD-domain.md.template`

# {Domain} — Detailed Design

## Interview

Start by confirming ADR decisions[cite: 4]: *"ADR-001 mandates {Choice A}, ADR-002 mandates {Choice B}. Confirming these directives govern this design."* Questions are asked ONE at a time[cite: 4]. The agent operates as a Lead Systems Engineer[cite: 4].

**Required:**
1. **Domain Models & Aggregate Boundaries (DDD)** — "What are the Aggregate Roots, Entities, and Value Objects for this domain? For each: attributes with explicit types, invariants (business validation rules), and domain events emitted?"
2. **Database Schema & DDL Migration** — "What is the concrete database schema? Provide raw SQL DDL (or document schema) including tables, column types, NOT NULL / DEFAULT constraints, foreign keys, and indexes."
3. **API Contracts & Interface Signatures** — "What are the concrete API endpoints or RPC methods? Provide request/response shapes, header requirements, authentication scopes, and validation rules."
4. **Project Structure & Scaffold Modules** — "What modules will be generated in `src/`? For each: module directory name, scaffold identifier (`go-service`, `python-fastapi`, `react-vite`, `rust-service`, `node-service`), and list of domain entities."[cite: 4]

**Optional / Operational:**
5. **Error Taxonomy & HTTP/gRPC Status Codes** — "What is the domain error taxonomy? List domain error codes, client-facing messages, HTTP/gRPC status codes, and whether the client should retry."
6. **Repository & Service Interface Signatures** — "What are the exact method signatures for the domain service and persistence repository interfaces?"

## Output

---
id: LLD-{NNN}-{slug}
domain: {domain}
stage: lld
version: 1.0.0
status: approved
created_at: {TIMESTAMP}

# {Domain} — Low-Level Design Specification

## 1. Domain-Driven Design (DDD) Models

### 1.1 Aggregate Root: `{AggregateName}`
* **Description:** <!-- Core domain entity managing transaction consistency boundary -->
* **Invariants & Validation Rules:**
  * Invariant 1: <!-- e.g., Balance must never be negative -->
  * Invariant 2: <!-- e.g., State transitions must follow: DRAFT -> ACTIVE -> CLOSED -->
* **Domain Events Emitted:**
  * `{AggregateName}CreatedEvent`
  * `{AggregateName}UpdatedEvent`

```typescript
// Type Definition: Aggregate Root & Value Objects
export interface {AggregateName} {
  id: string; // UUIDv7
  tenantId: string;
  status: '{AggregateName}Status';
  createdAt: string; // ISO 8601 UTC
  updatedAt: string; // ISO 8601 UTC
}

export type {AggregateName}Status = 'DRAFT' | 'ACTIVE' | 'ARCHIVED';

## 2. Database Schema & DDL Migrations

```sql
-- Migration: 000001_create_{domain}_tables.up.sql
-- Dialect: PostgreSQL 16+

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {domain}_aggregates (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'DRAFT',
    metadata JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {domain}_items (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    aggregate_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES {domain}_aggregates(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    quantity INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (quantity >= 0),
    unit_price NUMERIC(12, 4) NOT NULL CHECK (unit_price >= 0),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Optimization & Query Indexes
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_{domain}_aggregates_tenant_status 
    ON {domain}_aggregates (tenant_id, status);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_{domain}_items_aggregate_id 
    ON {domain}_items (aggregate_id);

```

## 3. API Contracts (OpenAPI 3.1 Specification)

```yaml
openapi: 3.1.0
info:
  title: {Domain} Service API
  version: 1.0.0
paths:
  /api/v1/{domain}:
    post:
      summary: Create a new {domain} aggregate
      operationId: create{Domain}
      parameters:
        - in: header
          name: X-Idempotency-Key
          schema:
            type: string
          required: false
          description: Client-generated idempotency key
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/Create{Domain}Request'
      responses:
        '201':
          description: Successfully created
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/{Domain}Response'
        '400':
          $ref: '#/components/responses/ValidationError'
        '409':
          $ref: '#/components/responses/ConflictError'

components:
  schemas:
    Create{Domain}Request:
      type: object
      required:
        - tenantId
        - name
      properties:
        tenantId:
          type: string
          format: uuid
        name:
          type: string
          minLength: 3
          maxLength: 255
    {Domain}Response:
      type: object
      required:
        - id
        - status
        - createdAt
      properties:
        id:
          type: string
          format: uuid
        status:
          type: string
          enum: [DRAFT, ACTIVE, ARCHIVED]
        createdAt:
          type: string
          format: date-time

```

## 4. Interfaces & Ports (Hexagonal / Clean Architecture)

```go
// Package ports defines the domain contracts and storage interfaces
package ports

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
)

var (
	ErrNotFound = errors.New("{domain}: entity not found")
	ErrConflict = errors.New("{domain}: state conflict")
)

// DomainService specifies the core business operations
type DomainService interface {
	Create(ctx context.Context, req CreateDTO) (*DomainEntity, error)
	GetByID(ctx context.Context, id string) (*DomainEntity, error)
}

// Repository specifies the persistence contract (Outbound Port)
type Repository interface {
	Save(ctx context.Context, entity *DomainEntity) error
	FindByID(ctx context.Context, id string) (*DomainEntity, error)
}

```

---

## 5. Error Taxonomy & Status Code Matrix

| Error Identifier | Domain Condition | HTTP Status | gRPC Code | Client Retryable | User-Facing Message |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ERR_{DOMAIN}_NOT_FOUND` | Record does not exist for ID | 404 Not Found | `NOT_FOUND` | No | "The requested resource could not be found." |
| `ERR_CONCURRENCY_CONFLICT` | Version conflict / state changed | 409 Conflict | `ABORTED` | Yes (with backoff) | "Resource was updated by another request. Please retry." |
| `ERR_INVALID_PAYLOAD` | Validation invariant failed | 422 Unprocessable | `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | No | "Validation error: check input constraints." |
| `ERR_RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED` | Token bucket depleted | 429 Too Many Requests | `RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED` | Yes (after delay) | "Rate limit exceeded. Please wait." |

## Phase 6: Upgrading Stage 5 — Implementation & Code Scaffolding (`Code`)

In this phase, we elevate **`/genops-code`** and its corresponding template into a test-driven, compiler-verified implementation engine authored under the **Principal Software Engineer** persona.

---

### Key Super Skill Enhancements for Stage 5

1. **Principal Software Engineer Persona:** Enforces strict Object-Oriented Design (OOD/OOP), SOLID principles, Clean/Hexagonal Architecture boundaries, type safety, and zero compiler warnings.


2. **TDD-First Scaffolding Sequence (Red $\to$ Green $\to$ Refactor):** Rather than generating raw application code directly, the skill first scaffolds domain invariants, value objects, and **failing unit/contract test suites** asserting the API and database constraints specified in LLD.


3. **Compiler-in-the-Loop Verification:** Executes installed language toolchains (`go test -run=^$`, `tsc --noEmit`, `cargo check`, `pytest`) to verify syntax, type safety, and package boundaries before prompting the user.


4. **Automated CI/CD Anti-Drift Check:** Runs `genops drift` to assert 100% synchronization between LLD declared entities and the generated file tree in `src/`.


5. **Cross-Module Infrastructure Orchestration:** Automatically configures containerization targets (`Dockerfile`, `docker-compose.yml`) with inter-service networking, environment variable propagation, and health check routes.



---

### 1. Upgraded Skill: `.agents/skills/genops-code/SKILL.md`

```markdown
---
name: genops-code
description: Use when scaffolding project structure, generating stub files, tests, and build configs from LLD design. Terminal stage in the GenOps pipeline. Requires completed LLD.
---

# Implementation (Code) — Principal Software Engineer Persona

Terminal stage in the software specification pipeline. Reads LLD specifications and scaffolds production-grade source code, domain models, and test harnesses into `src/`[cite: 4].

**Cognitive Role:** Principal Software Engineer. Clean Architecture purist, TDD practitioner, compiler-driven, and zero-drift enforcer.

**Config:** `id: code`, `requires: [docs/lld/]`, `outputs: src/`, `template: code/CODE-domain.md.template`, `next: []`[cite: 4]

## Execution Protocol

### 1. PRE-FLIGHT
- Verify `docs/lld/` exists with ≥1 approved LLD specification containing the `## 6. Project Structure & Scaffolding Blueprint` section and `### Modules` table[cite: 4].
- Verify all scaffold identifiers referenced in LLD exist in `.agents/scaffolds/<scaffold-id>/STRUCTURE.yaml`[cite: 4].
- Verify that previous stages (PRD, HLD, ADR, LLD) are marked as `approved` in `docs/.genops-state.json`[cite: 4].

### 2. LOAD
- Read all upstream `docs/lld/*.md` documents[cite: 4].
- Extract domain entities, database DDL, API contracts, error taxonomies, and the `### Modules` scaffolding table[cite: 4].
- Load scaffold definitions from `.agents/scaffolds/` for all target modules[cite: 4].

### 3. DOMAINS & MODULES
- Map each entry in LLD's `### Modules` table to a target directory in `src/<module>/`[cite: 4].
- If `--domain <slug>` is specified, limit scaffolding strictly to the module(s) implementing that domain[cite: 4].

### 4. CHECK
- Compute LF-normalized hash of `docs/lld/` via `python .agents/scripts/genops.py hash docs/lld/`[cite: 4].
- Check `docs/.genops-state.json` to verify LLD has not drifted[cite: 4].

### 5. INTERVIEW (Principal Software Engineer)
Ask interview questions ONE at a time[cite: 4]:
1. **Execution Mode:**
   - A) **Scaffold Baseline + TDD Test Harness:** Generates Clean Architecture folder layout, build files, entity models, and failing test suites[cite: 4].
   - B) **Full Implementation Plan + Code:** Generates complete implementation tasks, domain services, repository implementations, and HTTP handlers[cite: 4].
2. **Conflict Resolution:**
   - If `src/` contains existing code: "Merge changes, overwrite existing stubs, or isolate to new files?"[cite: 4]
3. **Module Confirmation:**
   - "Confirming generation of modules: `{module_list}`. Proceed with full scaffold?"[cite: 4]

### 6. GENERATE (Deterministic Polyglot Scaffolding)

For each module declared in LLD:
1. **Execute Deterministic Scaffolder:**
   ```bash
   python .agents/scripts/genops.py scaffold --module {module} --scaffold {scaffold_id} --entities {entity_list}

```

2. **Generate Domain Models & Invariants:**
* Implement constructor functions with validation (e.g., `NewAggregate(...)`).
* Implement value objects and domain error types.


3. **Generate Failing TDD Test Suite (Red State):**
* Implement unit tests asserting all domain invariants specified in LLD Section 1.


* Implement contract test fixtures asserting OpenAPI/gRPC schema shapes specified in LLD Section 3.




4. **Generate Orchestration & Container Files:**
* If >1 module exists: generate/update root `docker-compose.yml` connecting service dependencies and database instances.


* Generate language-appropriate `.gitignore` and `README.md` in `src/`.





### 7. VALIDATE (Compiler Feedback & Anti-Drift Gate)

1. **Automated Compiler & Linter Verification:**
* **Go:** `go vet ./...` and `go test -run=^$`

* **TypeScript / React:** `tsc --noEmit`

* **Python:** `ruff check .` or `python -m py_compile`

* **Rust:** `cargo check`


*Self-Healing Loop:* If compilation fails, analyze diagnostics and fix syntax/type errors immediately before presenting.


2. **Run CI Anti-Drift Gate:**
```bash
python .agents/scripts/genops.py drift

```


Assert that 100% of LLD-declared modules and entity stubs are present with zero drift.



### 8. PRESENT → APPROVE

* Present the file tree of generated packages, tests, build configurations, and compilation status.


* Enforce hard confirmation gate (`<HARD-GATE>`).



### 9. RECORD & AUDIT

* Run `python .agents/scripts/genops.py record code --actor user`.


* Atomically record output hashes into `docs/.genops-state.json` and append an immutable event to `docs/.genops-events.jsonl`.



### 10. TRANSITION

* Terminal stage. Announce: *"Pipeline execution complete. All modules scaffolded and verified in src/."*
